````diff
 /// is the exact largest `len` for which `8*len + 8` still fits in an `i64`.
 pub const MAX_ARRAY_LEN: i64 = (i64::MAX - 8) / 8;
 
+/// What an [`RtFunc`] is on the WASM target (spec
+/// `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md`
+/// §4.6).
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum WasmRuntime {
+    /// A function of `std/rt`, by its name there: MIR calls it directly
+    /// (`lower_module_with`).
+    Nova(&'static str),
+    /// An import from the shim's `env`, by its field name.
+    Host(&'static str),
+    /// Nothing on WASM: a program that needs it is E0091.
+    Unavailable,
+}
+
+impl RtFunc {
+    /// This runtime function on the WASM target. Exhaustive, so a new
+    /// runtime function has to decide what it is there.
+    pub fn on_wasm(self) -> WasmRuntime {
+        match self {
+            RtFunc::Println => WasmRuntime::Host("println"),
+            RtFunc::Print => WasmRuntime::Host("print"),
+            RtFunc::EPrint => WasmRuntime::Host("eprint"),
+            RtFunc::EPrintln => WasmRuntime::Host("eprintln"),
+            RtFunc::StrConcat => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_concat"),
+            RtFunc::StrConcatN => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_concat_n"),
+            RtFunc::IntToStr => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_int_to_str"),
+            RtFunc::FloatToStr => WasmRuntime::Host("float_to_str"),
+            RtFunc::FloatFixed => WasmRuntime::Host("float_fixed"),
+            RtFunc::StrToFloat => WasmRuntime::Host("str_to_float"),
+            RtFunc::BoolToStr => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bool_to_str"),
+            RtFunc::CharToStr => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_char_to_str"),
+            RtFunc::StrEq => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_eq"),
+            RtFunc::StrCmp => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_cmp"),
+            RtFunc::StrHash => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_hash"),
+            RtFunc::StrLenChars => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_len_chars"),
+            RtFunc::StrChars => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_chars"),
+            RtFunc::StrFromChars => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_from_chars"),
+            RtFunc::StrJoin => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_join"),
+            RtFunc::StrIndexOf => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_str_index_of"),
+            RtFunc::JsonQuote => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_json_quote"),
+            RtFunc::StrToUpper => WasmRuntime::Host("str_to_upper"),
+            RtFunc::StrToLower => WasmRuntime::Host("str_to_lower"),
+            RtFunc::Alloc => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_alloc"),
+            RtFunc::CheckBounds => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_check_bounds"),
+            RtFunc::Panic => WasmRuntime::Host("panic"),
+            RtFunc::BytesLen => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_len"),
+            RtFunc::BytesFromString => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_from_string"),
+            RtFunc::BytesIsUtf8 => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_is_utf8"),
+            RtFunc::BytesToStringUnchecked => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_to_string_unchecked"),
+            RtFunc::BytesAt => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_at"),
+            RtFunc::BytesSlice => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_slice"),
+            RtFunc::BytesConcat => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_concat"),
+            RtFunc::BytesToInts => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_to_ints"),
+            RtFunc::BytesFromInts => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_from_ints"),
+            RtFunc::BytesEq => WasmRuntime::Nova("rt_bytes_eq"),
+            RtFunc::IntHashSeed => WasmRuntime::Host("int_hash_seed"),
+            RtFunc::TestSelector
+            | RtFunc::TaskSpawn
+            | RtFunc::TaskBlockOn
+            | RtFunc::TaskIsDone
+            | RtFunc::TaskTakeOutput
````

### Task 3: Routing to `std/rt`, E0091, `std/rt`'s string basics

Spec §3.3, §5.3 and §5.4; decisions 7 and 15.

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `crates/nova-mir/src/{lib,mono,async_lower}.rs`
  - `crates/nova-driver/src/lib.rs`
  - `std/rt/lib.nova`
- Test:
  - `crates/nova-mir/tests/lower_tests.rs`
  - `crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs`

- [ ] **Step 1: The tests.** `patch_block t3-tests`.
  - `lower_tests.rs` gains `wasm_mir_for(src)`. It resolves against
    WASM's std, finds `$std.rt`'s functions by name as the driver does,
    and lowers with `lower_module_with`. Its three tests:
    - `a_wasm_build_calls_std_rt_where_a_native_build_calls_the_runtime`:
      `IntToStr` and `StrConcat` become calls to `rt_int_to_str.` and
      `rt_str_concat.`, which join the module with `rt_str_alloc.` and
      `rt_alloc.`. `println` stays a `CallRuntime`. `runtime_alloc`
      names the allocator, and natively nothing changes.
    - `a_wasm_build_holds_only_the_runtime_functions_it_reaches`.
    - `a_runtime_function_wasm_lacks_is_e0091`: `async fn main` needs
      `task_block_on`, and only that.
  - `wasm_tests.rs` gains
    `check_for_wasm_reports_a_runtime_function_wasm_lacks`, from the CLI.
- [ ] **Step 2: Run, and see them fail.**
  - `cargo test -p nova-mir --test lower_tests`. Expected: it does not
    compile, with five errors:
    - ``cannot find `RuntimeImpls` in `nova_mir` ``;
    - ``cannot find function `lower_module_with` ``;
    - ``cannot find struct, variant or union type `Options` ``;
    - ``no field `runtime_alloc` ``, twice.
  - `NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE=1 cargo test -p nova-cli --test wasm_tests`.
    Expected: `4 passed; 1 failed`. The new test reports `Unexpected
    success`, with `ok: main.nova`.
- [ ] **Step 3: The code.** `patch_block t3-code`:
  - **`nova-mir`:**
    - `RuntimeImpls`, `Options` and `lower_module_with`;
      `lower_module` calls it with the defaults.
    - With `Options::runtime` set:
      - `rt_alloc` is a root, named in `Module::runtime_alloc`;
      - each lowered function's calls to a `WasmRuntime::Nova` runtime
        function become `Call`s to its `std/rt` function, which joins the
        worklist;
      - after the async transform, the routing runs once more.
    - Then E0091, once per runtime function still called that is not the
      shim's: "this program needs `{name}`, which target wasm does not
      have", at `main`, "in the program this `main` starts".
  - **`nova-driver`:**
    - `runtime_impls`: `$std.rt`'s functions by name;
    - `mir_options`;
    - the MIR lowering `check` already runs takes those options, so
      `check --target wasm` reports E0091.
  - **`std/rt/lib.nova`:**
    - `str_len`, `str_bytes`;
    - `rt_str_alloc`, `rt_str_concat`, `rt_str_concat_n`;
    - `rt_int_to_str`.

    A `std/rt` function takes and returns `Int` addresses where the
    native runtime function takes pointers.
- [ ] **Step 4: Run.**
  - The same two commands. Expected: `57 passed` and `5 passed`.
  - Then `cargo test --no-fail-fast -p nova-mir -p nova-driver -p

- [ ] **Step 5: Checks,** as Task 1's Step 5.

````diff
         stderr(&a)
     );
 }
+
+/// A program that needs a runtime function WASM lacks is E0091, from
+/// `check` as from `build` (spec §3.3).
+#[test]
+fn check_for_wasm_reports_a_runtime_function_wasm_lacks() {
+    let dir = fresh("check-async");
+    write(
+        &dir,
+        &[("main.nova", "async fn main() { println(\"a\") }\n")],
+    );
+    let a = nova_in(&dir, &["check", "--target", "wasm", "main.nova"]).failure();
+    assert!(
+        stderr(&a).contains(
+            "error[E0091]: this program needs `task_block_on`, which target wasm does not have"
+        ),
+        "{}",
+        stderr(&a)
+    );
+    nova_in(&dir, &["check", "main.nova"]).success();
+}
diff --git a/crates/nova-mir/tests/lower_tests.rs b/crates/nova-mir/tests/lower_tests.rs
index 9340da8..4cc8dd5 100644
--- a/crates/nova-mir/tests/lower_tests.rs
+++ b/crates/nova-mir/tests/lower_tests.rs
@@ -1806,3 +1806,138 @@ fn an_async_main_is_wrapped_in_a_shim_that_drives_it() {
         "the user's body must still have been split into a poll fn: {names:?}"
     );
 }
+
+/// `src` lowered for WASM (spec
+/// `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md`
+/// §5.4): resolved against WASM's std, with `std/rt`'s functions found by
+/// name as the driver finds them.
+fn wasm_mir_for(src: &str) -> Result<nova_mir::Module, Vec<nova_diagnostics::Diagnostic>> {
+    use nova_resolver::{CompileTarget, Def, DefKind, ModuleSource};
+    let (tokens, lex_errors) = lex(src, FileId::DUMMY);
+    assert!(lex_errors.is_empty(), "lex errors: {lex_errors:?}");
+    let (ast, parse_errors) = parse(&tokens, FileId::DUMMY);
+    assert!(parse_errors.is_empty(), "parse errors: {parse_errors:?}");
+    let ast = ast.expect("no AST");
+    let mut sources = [ModuleSource::new("main", &ast)];
+    nova_resolver::name_imports(&mut sources);
+    let std_files: Vec<FileId> = CompileTarget::Wasm
+        .std_modules()
+        .iter()
+        .map(|_| FileId::DUMMY)
+        .collect();
+    let resolved =
+        nova_resolver::resolve_program_for(CompileTarget::Wasm, &sources, &std_files, None);
+    assert!(
+        resolved.diagnostics.is_empty(),
+        "resolve: {:?}",
+        resolved.diagnostics
+    );
+    let checked = check(&resolved.file, &resolved.definitions);
+    assert!(
+        checked.diagnostics.is_empty(),
+        "typeck: {:?}",
+        checked.diagnostics
+    );
+    let defs = &resolved.definitions;
+    let mut impls = nova_mir::RuntimeImpls::default();
+    for f in &checked.module.functions {
+        if let Some(Def {
+            kind: DefKind::Fn { item_index },
+            ..
+        }) = defs.defs().get(f.def_id.0 as usize)
+        {
+            if defs.module_name(defs.module_of(*item_index)) == "$std.rt" {
+                impls.functions.insert(f.name.clone(), f.def_id);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    nova_mir::lower_module_with(
+        &checked.module,
+        &nova_mir::Options {
+            runtime: Some(&impls),
+        },
+    )
+}
+
+/// On WASM, a runtime function `std/rt` implements is a direct call to it,
+/// and the module gains that function; the shim's stay `CallRuntime`s; the
+/// allocator is a root, named in `runtime_alloc` (spec 4.0 §5.4).
+#[test]
+fn a_wasm_build_calls_std_rt_where_a_native_build_calls_the_runtime() {
+    let src = "fn main() { println(\"n = ${7}\") }";
+    let mir = wasm_mir_for(src).expect("lowers for WASM");
+    let main = stmts_of(&mir, "main");
+    assert_eq!(count_rt(&main, nova_mir::RtFunc::IntToStr), 0);
+    assert_eq!(count_rt(&main, nova_mir::RtFunc::StrConcat), 0);
+    assert_eq!(count_rt(&main, nova_mir::RtFunc::Println), 1);
+    let calls: Vec<&str> = main
+        .iter()
+        .filter_map(|s| match s {
+            nova_mir::Stmt::Call { callee, .. } => Some(callee.as_str()),
+            _ => None,
+        })
+        .collect();
+    assert!(
+        calls.iter().any(|c| c.starts_with("rt_int_to_str.")),
+        "{calls:?}"
+    );
+    assert!(
+        calls.iter().any(|c| c.starts_with("rt_str_concat.")),
+        "{calls:?}"
+    );
+    let names = function_names(&mir);
+    for f in [
+        "rt_int_to_str.",
+        "rt_str_concat.",
+        "rt_str_alloc.",
+        "rt_alloc.",
+    ] {
+        assert!(names.iter().any(|n| n.starts_with(f)), "{f}: {names:?}");
+    }
+    let alloc = mir
+        .runtime_alloc
+        .as_deref()
+        .expect("the allocator is named");
+    assert!(alloc.starts_with("rt_alloc."), "{alloc}");
+    assert!(names.contains(&alloc), "{names:?}");
+
+    // Natively, nothing changes.
+    let native = mir_for(src);
+    assert_eq!(
+        count_rt(&stmts_of(&native, "main"), nova_mir::RtFunc::IntToStr),
+        1
+    );
+    assert_eq!(native.runtime_alloc, None);
+}
+
+/// Only what the program reaches joins it: a literal needs no `Int` text.
+#[test]
+fn a_wasm_build_holds_only_the_runtime_functions_it_reaches() {
+    let mir = wasm_mir_for("fn main() { println(\"hi\") }").expect("lowers for WASM");
+    let names = function_names(&mir);
+    assert!(
+        names.iter().any(|n| n.starts_with("rt_alloc.")),
+        "{names:?}"
+    );
+    assert!(
+        !names.iter().any(|n| n.starts_with("rt_int_to_str.")),
+        "{names:?}"
+    );
+}
+
+/// A runtime function WASM lacks is E0091, naming it (spec 4.0 §3.3).
+#[test]
+fn a_runtime_function_wasm_lacks_is_e0091() {
+    let diags = wasm_mir_for("async fn main() { println(\"a\") }").expect_err("refused");
+    let messages: Vec<(&str, &str)> = diags
+        .iter()
+        .map(|d| (d.code.as_str(), d.message.as_str()))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        messages,
+        [(
+            "E0091",
+            "this program needs `task_block_on`, which target wasm does not have"
+        )]
+    );
+}
````

````diff
+/// Turn each of `f`'s calls to a [`WasmRuntime::Nova`] runtime function into
+/// a call to its `std/rt` function, returning those functions for the
+/// worklist (spec 4.0 §5.4). A name `impls` lacks stays a `CallRuntime`, and
+/// [`unavailable_on_wasm`] reports it.
+fn route_runtime(f: &mut Function, impls: &RuntimeImpls, module: &hir::Module) -> Vec<DefId> {
+    let mut needed = Vec::new();
+    for block in &mut f.blocks {
+        for stmt in &mut block.stmts {
+            let Stmt::CallRuntime { dst, func, args } = stmt else {
+                continue;
+            };
+            let WasmRuntime::Nova(name) = func.on_wasm() else {
+                continue;
+            };
+            let Some((&def, hir_name)) = impls
+                .functions
+                .get(name)
+                .and_then(|def| module.function(*def).map(|g| (def, &g.name)))
+            else {
+                continue;
+            };
+            *stmt = Stmt::Call {
+                dst: *dst,
+                callee: mangle(def, hir_name, &[]),
+                args: std::mem::take(args),
+            };
+            needed.push(def);
+        }
+    }
+    needed
+}
+
+/// E0091 for each runtime function the module still calls that is neither
+/// the shim's nor routed to `std/rt` (spec 4.0 §3.3), once per function.
+fn unavailable_on_wasm(mir: &Module, main_span: nova_diagnostics::Span) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
+    let mut seen: Vec<RtFunc> = Vec::new();
+    for stmt in mir
+        .functions
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|f| &f.blocks)
+        .flat_map(|b| &b.stmts)
+    {
+        if let Stmt::CallRuntime { func, .. } = stmt {
+            if !matches!(func.on_wasm(), WasmRuntime::Host(_)) && !seen.contains(func) {
+                seen.push(*func);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    seen.iter()
+        .map(|func| {
+            let name = func.symbol().trim_start_matches("nova_rt_");
+            Diagnostic::error(
+                "E0091",
+                format!("this program needs `{name}`, which target wasm does not have"),
+            )
+            .with_primary_label(main_span, "in the program this `main` starts")
+        })
+        .collect()
+}
+

 /// `wrapper`, then hand the future to the executor.
 ///
diff --git a/std/rt/lib.nova b/std/rt/lib.nova
index 28e4b33..92656ad 100644
--- a/std/rt/lib.nova
+++ b/std/rt/lib.nova
@@ -32,3 +32,85 @@ fn rt_alloc(size: Int) -> Int {
     mem_store64(HEAP_NEXT, end)
     at
 }
+
+// A string's length in bytes.
+fn str_len(s: Int) -> Int { mem_load64(s) }
+
+// The address of a string's bytes. The header keeps it in the low half of
+// its second word, the high half zero.
+fn str_bytes(s: Int) -> Int { mem_load64(s + 8) }
+
+// A header for `len` bytes, with the bytes inline at offset 16, as
+// `nova-runtime`'s `alloc_str_object`. Room for one byte at least, so an
+// empty string's bytes are still its own.
+fn rt_str_alloc(len: Int) -> Int {
+    let mut room = len
+    if room < 1 { room = 1 }
+    let h = rt_alloc(16 + room)
+    mem_store64(h, len)
+    mem_store64(h + 8, h + 16)
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_concat`.
+fn rt_str_concat(a: Int, b: Int) -> Int {
+    let la = str_len(a)
+    let lb = str_len(b)
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(la + lb)
+    let at = str_bytes(h)
+    mem_copy(at, str_bytes(a), la)
+    mem_copy(at + la, str_bytes(b), lb)
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_concat_n`: every string of the array `parts`, in order. A
+// negative length counts as zero.
+fn rt_str_concat_n(parts: Int) -> Int {
+    let mut n = mem_load64(parts)
+    if n < 0 { n = 0 }
+    let mut total = 0
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        total = total + str_len(mem_load64(parts + 8 + 8 * i))
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(total)
+    let mut at = str_bytes(h)
+    i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        let part = mem_load64(parts + 8 + 8 * i)
+        let len = str_len(part)
+        mem_copy(at, str_bytes(part), len)
+        at = at + len
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_int_to_str`. The digits are counted and written on the negative
+// side, where `i64::MIN` still has a value.
+fn rt_int_to_str(v: Int) -> Int {
+    let negative = v < 0
+    let mut rest = v
+    if !negative { rest = -v }
+    let mut digits = 1
+    let mut t = rest / 10
+    while t != 0 {
+        digits = digits + 1
+        t = t / 10
+    }
+    let mut len = digits
+    if negative { len = len + 1 }
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(len)
+    let start = str_bytes(h)
+    let mut at = start + len
+    let mut k = 0
+    while k < digits {
+        at = at - 1
+        mem_store8(at, 48 - rest % 10)
+        rest = rest / 10
+        k = k + 1
+    }
+    if negative { mem_store8(start, 45) }
+    h
+}
````

### Task 4: Heap objects, literals, closures, conversions

Spec §4.3 to §4.5, and §5.4's conversions; decisions 10 and 11. The
tests are unit tests in `lib.rs`. Task 10's mutant 3 shows that they bite;
mutant 12 reaches the same code through the CLI.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/lib.rs`

- [ ] **Step 1: The code.** `patch_block t4-code`.
  - **Heap statements:**
    - records, sums, arrays and closures are made through
      `runtime_alloc`;
    - fields and elements are read and written at 8-byte slots;
    - a `Ptr` is stored zero-extended (`i64.extend_i32_u`), so a slot
      `std/rt` reads whole holds the address.
  - **String literals:** each distinct one is a static 16-byte header,
    `{len: i64, ptr: i32, 0: i32}`, then its bytes, in the data segment
    from `DATA_BASE`.
  - **Closures:**
    - a function value is an index into a `funcref` table, and an
      environment;
    - `CallIndirect` is `call_indirect` on the type `(env i32, params…)
      → ret`;
    - the types are interned before any function is lowered.
  - **At a call to a `std/rt` function,** a `Ptr` argument becomes an
    `Int` (`i64.extend_i32_u`), and an `Int` result becomes a `Ptr`
    (`i32.wrap_i64`).
  - `nova_alloc` is exported when `runtime_alloc` is set.
  - **Five tests:**
    - `heap_objects_lower_through_the_allocator`;
    - `a_string_literal_is_a_static_header`;
    - `a_closure_is_a_table_index_and_an_environment`;
    - `a_call_into_std_rt_converts_between_ptr_and_int`;
    - `a_heap_object_without_an_allocator_is_refused`.
- [ ] **Step 2: Run** `cargo test --no-fail-fast -p nova-codegen-wasm`.
  Expected: `19 passed`.
- [ ] **Step 3: Checks,** as Task 1's Step 5, without `nova fmt`.
- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**
  `wasm: heap objects, static string literals, closures, conversions at std/rt calls`.

**Patch `t4-code`:**

````diff
+    literals: HashMap<String, u32>,

+
+/// A `call_indirect`'s type: the environment pointer, then `params`.
+fn indirect_type(params: &[MirTy], ret: MirTy) -> (Vec<ValType>, Vec<ValType>) {
+    let mut abi = vec![ValType::I32];
+    abi.extend(vals(params));
+    (abi, val(ret).into_iter().collect())
+}
+
+/// The data segment: each distinct string literal's 16-byte header,
+/// `{len: i64, ptr: i32, 0: i32}`, then its bytes, each part 8-aligned,
+/// from [`DATA_BASE`] (spec §4.5). Returns the bytes and each header's
+/// address.
+fn literal_data(mir: &Module) -> (Vec<u8>, HashMap<String, u32>) {
+    let mut data: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
+    let mut at: HashMap<String, u32> = HashMap::new();
+    for stmt in mir
+        .functions
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|f| &f.blocks)
+        .flat_map(|b| &b.stmts)
+    {
+        let Stmt::ConstStr(_, text) = stmt else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        if at.contains_key(text) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let header = DATA_BASE + data.len() as u32;
+        let bytes = header + 16;
+        data.extend_from_slice(&(text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes());
+        data.extend_from_slice(&bytes.to_le_bytes());
+        data.extend_from_slice(&[0; 4]);
+        data.extend_from_slice(text.as_bytes());
+        while data.len() % 8 != 0 {
+            data.push(0);
+        }
+        at.insert(text.clone(), header);
+    }
+    (data, at)
 }

+    /// Convert the value on the stack from `from`'s class to `to`'s, where a
+    /// `Ptr` meets an `Int` address (spec §5.4); otherwise nothing.
+    fn convert(&mut self, from: MirTy, to: MirTy) {
+        match (from, to) {
+            (MirTy::Ptr, MirTy::I64) => self.out.push(I::I64ExtendI32U),
+            (MirTy::I64, MirTy::Ptr) => self.out.push(I::I32WrapI64),
+            _ => {}
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn alloc_index(&self) -> Result<u32> {
+        self.layout
+            .alloc
+            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("a heap object, but the module has no allocator"))
+    }
+
+    /// Allocate `size` bytes; the object's address is left on the stack.
+    fn alloc(&mut self, size: i64) -> Result<()> {
+        let alloc = self.alloc_index()?;
+        self.out.push(I::I64Const(size));
+        self.out.push(I::Call(alloc));
+        self.out.push(I::I32WrapI64);
+        Ok(())
+    }
+
+    /// `arr + 8*index`, the address of element `index` less its 8-byte
+    /// header, as an `i32`.
+    fn elem_addr(&mut self, arr: Temp, index: Temp) {
+        self.get(arr);
+        self.get(index);
+        self.out.push(I::I32WrapI64);
+        self.out.push(I::I32Const(3));
+        self.out.push(I::I32Shl);
+        self.out.push(I::I32Add);
+    }
+
+    /// Store `v` of class `ty` at `object + offset`.
+    fn store_field(&mut self, object: Temp, offset: u64, v: Temp, ty: MirTy) {
+        if let Some(i) = self.locals[object.0 as usize] {
+            self.store_at(i, offset, v, ty);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Store `v` of class `ty` at `local + offset`, `local` an `i32`
+    /// address. A `Ptr` is stored zero-extended to 8 bytes (spec §4.1).
+    fn store_at(&mut self, local: u32, offset: u64, v: Temp, ty: MirTy) {
+        if val(ty).is_none() {
+            return;
+        }
+        self.out.push(I::LocalGet(local));
+        self.get(v);
+        match ty {
+            MirTy::I64 => self.out.push(I::I64Store(mem(offset, 3))),
+            MirTy::F64 => self.out.push(I::F64Store(mem(offset, 3))),
+            MirTy::I8 => self.out.push(I::I32Store8(mem(offset, 0))),
+            MirTy::Ptr => {
+                self.out.push(I::I64ExtendI32U);
+                self.out.push(I::I64Store(mem(offset, 3)));
+            }
+            MirTy::Unit => unreachable!("handled above"),
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Load `object + offset` of class `ty` into `dst`.
+    fn load_field(&mut self, dst: Temp, object: Temp, offset: u64, ty: MirTy) {
+        if let Some(i) = self.locals[object.0 as usize] {
+            self.load_at(dst, i, offset, ty);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Load `local + offset` of class `ty` into `dst`.
+    fn load_at(&mut self, dst: Temp, local: u32, offset: u64, ty: MirTy) {
+        if val(ty).is_none() || self.locals[dst.0 as usize].is_none() {
+            return;
+        }
+        self.out.push(I::LocalGet(local));
+        self.out.push(match ty {
+            MirTy::I64 => I::I64Load(mem(offset, 3)),
+            MirTy::F64 => I::F64Load(mem(offset, 3)),
+            MirTy::I8 => I::I32Load8U(mem(offset, 0)),
+            MirTy::Ptr => I::I32Load(mem(offset, 2)),
+            MirTy::Unit => unreachable!("handled above"),
+        });
+        self.set(dst);
+    }

+    fn alloc_fn() -> MirFn {

+            )],

+    }

+    fn with_alloc(main: MirFn) -> Module {

+    }

+        let data: Vec<(u32, Vec<u8>)> = wasmparser::Parser::new(0)
+            .parse_all(&out.wasm)
+            .filter_map(Result::ok)
+            .filter_map(|p| match p {
````

### Task 5: The release set: `nova-codegen-wasm` published

Spec §8; decision 1. From this task `nova-driver` depends on
`nova-codegen-wasm`, which Task 6's code uses, and a published crate
cannot depend on an unpublished one.

  - `.github/workflows/{ci,publish-crates}.yml`
- Create (copied): `crates/nova-codegen-wasm/LICENSE-{MIT,APACHE}`
- Generated: `Cargo.lock`
- Test: `crates/nova-cli/tests/{release_manifests,publish_script}.rs`

### Task 6: `build --target wasm`, `bundle`, `dist/`, the shim, Node in CI

Spec §3.1, §6, §7, §9.1 and §9.5; decisions 12, 13, 16, 17 and 20.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim.rs`
  - `crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/{core.js,run.js,index.html}`
- Modify:
  - `crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/lib.rs`
  - `crates/nova-driver/src/lib.rs`
  - `crates/nova-cli/src/{main.rs,cmd/run.rs}`
  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
- Test:
  - `crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs`
  - `crates/nova-cli/Cargo.toml`: the dev-dependency `flate2`. It is in
    `nova-cli`'s lockfile list already, as a build dependency, so the
    lockfile does not change.

- [ ] **Step 1: The tests.** `patch_block t6-tests`. `wasm_tests.rs`
  gains these helpers:
  - `repo_root()`;
  - `node()`: `None` when `node` is not on `PATH`, and a failure instead
    under `NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE=1`;
  - `build_wasm(dir, file)`: `nova build --target wasm FILE -o dir/dist`;
  - `run_wasm(node, dist)`: `node dist/app.js`.

  It also gains eight tests:
  - `build_for_wasm_writes_dist`:
    - `nova build --target wasm main.nova` prints `built dist`;
    - `index.html` imports `./app.js`;
    - `app.js` exports `init`;
    - `app.wasm` starts with WASM's magic number.
  - `bundle_is_build_for_wasm`: `nova bundle main.nova -o out` prints
    `built out`, and a file already in `out/` stays.
  - `a_project_builds_into_dist_at_its_root`, run from `src/`.
  - `release_does_not_apply_to_wasm`.
  - `hello_world_runs_under_node`: `Hello, World!`.
  - `hello_world_is_under_30_kb_gzipped`: `app.wasm` and `app.js`
    together.
  - `a_panic_exits_134_with_its_message`: `before`, then `nova: panic:
    boom`.
  - `a_trap_exits_132`: a division by zero, `nova: trap: divide by zero`.
- [ ] **Step 2: Run, and see them fail.**
  `NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE=1 cargo test -p nova-cli --test wasm_tests`.
  Expected: `5 passed; 8 failed`.
  - Seven fail with `unexpected argument '--target' found`.
  - `bundle_is_build_for_wasm` fails with `unrecognized subcommand
    'bundle'`.
- [ ] **Step 3: The code.** `patch_block t6-code`:
  - **`shim.rs`:**
    - `shim(imports) -> Result<String>` builds `app.js` from three
      parts: `core.js`; then `env`, one line per imported host
      function; then `run.js`.
    - An import with no entry is an error, "the shim has no host function
      `{name}`". This task's entries are `print`, `println`, `eprint`,
      `eprintln` and `panic`.
    - `INDEX_HTML` is the page.
    - Two unit tests.
  - **`core.js`:**
    - a string's text from its header, and a new string through
      `nova_alloc`;
    - output to Node's streams, or the console a line at a time;
    - `NovaPanic`.
  - **`run.js`:**
    - `export default async function init(options)`: it takes
      `options.wasm`, or loads `app.wasm` beside itself (`readFile` under
      Node, `instantiateStreaming` in a browser), then runs `main`.
    - A panic exits 134. A `RangeError` naming the call stack prints
      `nova: stack overflow` and exits 134. A `WebAssembly.RuntimeError`
      prints `nova: trap: <message>` and exits 132.
    - Run as `node app.js`, it calls `init()` itself.
  - **`nova-driver`:**
    - `build_program_wasm(program, out_dir)`: it lowers for WASM,
      compiles, and writes `index.html`, `app.js` and `app.wasm`;
    - `lower_to_mir_for`.
  - **`nova-cli`:**
    - `build --target <native|wasm>`; `-o` names the directory, which
      defaults to `dist/` at the project's root, or in the current
      directory for a file;
    - `--release` with `wasm` is an error, "`--release` does not apply to
      `--target wasm`";
    - `nova bundle [FILE] [-o DIR]`.
  - **`ci.yml`:** the test job installs Node 22 with
    `actions/setup-node@v4`, and runs `cargo test` with
    `NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE: "1"`.
- [ ] **Step 4: Run.**
  - `NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE=1 cargo test -p nova-cli --test wasm_tests`.
    Expected: `13 passed`.
  - `cargo test --no-fail-fast -p nova-codegen-wasm -p nova-driver -p
    nova-cli`, summed as in Task 0. Expected: `720 0 2`.
  - **By hand,** to see the page's parts. `nova` is not on `PATH`; the
    build is `target/debug/nova`:

    ```sh
    NOVA="$PWD/target/debug/nova"
    rm -rf "$P/hello-40" && mkdir -p "$P/hello-40" && cd "$P/hello-40"
    printf 'fn main() { println("Hello, World!") }\n' > main.nova
    "$NOVA" build --target wasm main.nova && node dist/app.js && ls dist
    cd - >/dev/null
    ```

    Expected: `built dist`, `Hello, World!`, and `app.js`, `app.wasm`
    and `index.html`.
- [ ] **Step 5: Checks,** as Task 1's Step 5, without `nova fmt`.
- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**
  `wasm: nova build --target wasm and nova bundle write dist/; the shim; Node in CI`.

**Patch `t6-tests`:**

<!-- patch t6-tests -->

````diff
diff --git a/crates/nova-cli/Cargo.toml b/crates/nova-cli/Cargo.toml
index 4a48aaa..8800df4 100644
--- a/crates/nova-cli/Cargo.toml
+++ b/crates/nova-cli/Cargo.toml
@@ -61,3 +61,7 @@ insta = { workspace = true }
 serde_json = { workspace = true }
 # The stand-in GitHub API the registry tests share with nova-index's.
 base64 = { workspace = true }
+# The WASM gate's size check (spec
+# docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md §9.5);
+# already a build dependency, so the lockfile does not change.
+flate2 = { workspace = true }
diff --git a/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs b/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
index e425d7e..578d4c6 100644
--- a/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
+++ b/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
@@ -142,3 +142,186 @@ fn check_for_wasm_reports_a_runtime_function_wasm_lacks() {
     );
     nova_in(&dir, &["check", "main.nova"]).success();
 }
+
+fn repo_root() -> PathBuf {
+    PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
+        .join("..")
+        .join("..")
+}
+
+/// `node`, or `None` when it is not on `PATH`, in which case the test
+/// passes without running; under `NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE=1`, as in CI, a missing
+/// `node` fails it instead (spec §9.1).
+fn node() -> Option<Command> {
+    let found = std::process::Command::new("node")
+        .arg("--version")
+        .output()
+        .map(|o| o.status.success())
+        .unwrap_or(false);
+    if found {
+        return Some(Command::new("node"));
+    }
+    assert_ne!(
+        std::env::var("NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE").as_deref(),
+        Ok("1"),
+        "NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE=1, but `node` is not on PATH"
+    );
+    eprintln!("skipped: `node` is not on PATH");
+    None
+}
+
+/// Build `file` for WASM into `dir/dist`, which must succeed.
+fn build_wasm(dir: &Path, file: &Path) -> PathBuf {
+    let dist = dir.join("dist");
+    let file = file.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
+    let out = dist.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
+    nova_in(dir, &["build", "--target", "wasm", &file, "-o", &out]).success();
+    dist
+}
+
+/// `node dist/app.js`, with no backtrace from `nova` involved.
+fn run_wasm(mut node: Command, dist: &Path) -> Assert {
+    node.arg(dist.join("app.js")).assert()
+}
+
+/// `dist/` holds the page, the shim and the module, and `nova build`
+/// names it (spec §7).
+#[test]
+fn build_for_wasm_writes_dist() {
+    let dir = fresh("build-dist");
+    write(&dir, &[("main.nova", "fn main() { println(\"hi\") }\n")]);
+    let a = nova_in(&dir, &["build", "--target", "wasm", "main.nova"]).success();
+    assert_eq!(stdout(&a), "built dist\n");
+    let dist = dir.join("dist");
+    let page = std::fs::read_to_string(dist.join("index.html")).expect("index.html");
+    assert!(page.contains("import init from \"./app.js\";"), "{page}");
+    let js = std::fs::read_to_string(dist.join("app.js")).expect("app.js");
+    assert!(js.contains("export default async function init("), "{js}");
+    let wasm = std::fs::read(dist.join("app.wasm")).expect("app.wasm");
+    assert_eq!(&wasm[..4], b"\0asm");
+}
+
+/// `nova bundle` is `nova build --target wasm`, and `-o` names the
+/// directory; anything else in it stays (spec §3.1, §7).
+#[test]
+fn bundle_is_build_for_wasm() {
+    let dir = fresh("bundle");
+    write(
+        &dir,
+        &[
+            ("main.nova", "fn main() { println(\"hi\") }\n"),
+            ("out/keep.txt", "kept"),
+        ],
+    );
+    let a = nova_in(&dir, &["bundle", "main.nova", "-o", "out"]).success();
+    assert_eq!(stdout(&a), "built out\n");
+    for name in ["index.html", "app.js", "app.wasm", "keep.txt"] {
+        assert!(dir.join("out").join(name).is_file(), "{name}");
+    }
+}
+
+/// In a project, `dist/` is at the project's root (spec §7).
+#[test]
+fn a_project_builds_into_dist_at_its_root() {
+    let dir = fresh("project");
+    write(
+        &dir,
+        &[
+            (
+                "nova.toml",
+                "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2026\"\n",
+            ),
+            ("src/main.nova", "fn main() { println(\"hi\") }\n"),
+        ],
+    );
+    nova_in(&dir.join("src"), &["build", "--target", "wasm"]).success();
+    assert!(dir.join("dist").join("app.wasm").is_file());
+}
+
+/// `--release` names the LLVM backend, which WASM does not use.
+#[test]
+fn release_does_not_apply_to_wasm() {
+    let dir = fresh("release");
+    write(&dir, &[("main.nova", "fn main() { println(\"hi\") }\n")]);
+    let a = nova_in(
+        &dir,
+        &["build", "--target", "wasm", "--release", "main.nova"],
+    )
+    .failure();
+    assert!(
+        stderr(&a).contains("`--release` does not apply to `--target wasm`"),
+        "{}",
+        stderr(&a)
+    );
+}
+
+/// The gate: hello world prints under Node what its native build prints
+/// (spec §1).
+#[test]
+fn hello_world_runs_under_node() {
+    let Some(node) = node() else { return };
+    let dir = fresh("hello");
+    let dist = build_wasm(
+        &dir,
+        &repo_root().join("examples/01-hello-world/src/main.nova"),
+    );
+    let a = run_wasm(node, &dist).success();
+    assert_eq!(stdout(&a), "Hello, World!\n");
+}
+
+/// The gate's size check: hello world's module and shim are under 30 KB
+/// gzipped (spec §9.5).
+#[test]
+fn hello_world_is_under_30_kb_gzipped() {
+    use std::io::Write;
+    let dir = fresh("hello-size");
+    let dist = build_wasm(
+        &dir,
+        &repo_root().join("examples/01-hello-world/src/main.nova"),
+    );
+    let mut total = 0;
+    for name in ["app.wasm", "app.js"] {
+        let bytes = std::fs::read(dist.join(name)).expect("read a dist file");
+        let mut gz = flate2::write::GzEncoder::new(Vec::new(), flate2::Compression::best());
+        gz.write_all(&bytes).expect("compress");
+        total += gz.finish().expect("compress").len();
+    }
+    println!("hello world: {total} bytes gzipped");
+    assert!(total < 30 * 1024, "{total} bytes gzipped");
+}
+
+/// A panic ends the program with its message and an abort's status, 134;
+/// what it printed first stays (spec §6).
+#[test]
+fn a_panic_exits_134_with_its_message() {
+    let Some(node) = node() else { return };
+    let dir = fresh("panic");
+    write(
+        &dir,
+        &[(
+            "main.nova",
+            "fn main() {\n    println(\"before\")\n    panic(\"boom\")\n}\n",
+        )],
+    );
+    let dist = build_wasm(&dir, Path::new("main.nova"));
+    let a = run_wasm(node, &dist).code(134);
+    assert_eq!(stdout(&a), "before\n");
+    assert_eq!(stderr(&a), "nova: panic: boom\n");
+}
+
+/// A trap, here a division by zero, ends it with a trap's status, 132.
+#[test]
+fn a_trap_exits_132() {
+    let Some(node) = node() else { return };
+    let dir = fresh("trap");
+    write(
+        &dir,
+        &[(
+            "main.nova",
+            "fn d(a: Int, b: Int) -> Int { a / b }\nfn main() { println(\"${d(1, 0)}\") }\n",
+        )],
+    );
+    let dist = build_wasm(&dir, Path::new("main.nova"));
+    let a = run_wasm(node, &dist).code(132);
+    assert_eq!(stderr(&a), "nova: trap: divide by zero\n");
+}
````

````diff
+/// `nova bundle`: `nova build --target wasm` (spec

+/// `nova build --target wasm`: `dist/` at the project's root, or in the
+/// current directory, unless `-o` names it (spec 4.0 §7).
+fn build_wasm(cmd: BuildCmd) -> Result<()> {
+    if cmd.release {
+        anyhow::bail!("`--release` does not apply to `--target wasm`");
+    }
+    let mode = project::mode(cmd.file)?;
+    project::refuse_library(&mode)?;
+    project::sync(&mode)?;
+    let program = project::program_to_run(&mode)?;
+    let output = match (cmd.output, &mode) {
+        (Some(output), _) => output,
+        (None, Mode::Project { root, .. }) => root.join("dist"),
+        (None, Mode::File(_)) => PathBuf::from("dist"),
+    };

+        Outcome::Ok(dir) => {
+            println!("built {}", dir.display());
+            Ok(())
+        }
+        Outcome::Failed { errors } => anyhow::bail!(
+            "could not compile due to {errors} previous error{}",
+            if errors == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
+        ),
+    }
+}

diff --git a/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim.rs b/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1b811ca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,59 @@
+//! `app.js` and `index.html` (spec
+//! `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md`
+//! §6, §7). `app.js` is the shim's core, then `env`, holding the host
+//! functions the module imports and no others, then the runner.
+
+use anyhow::{bail, Result};
+
+/// The page `dist/` holds: it loads `app.js` as a module and calls `init`.
+pub const INDEX_HTML: &str = include_str!("shim/index.html");
+
+const CORE: &str = include_str!("shim/core.js");
+const RUN: &str = include_str!("shim/run.js");
+
+/// A host function's entry in `env`, by its import name.
+fn host(name: &str) -> Result<&'static str> {
+    Ok(match name {
+        "print" => "  print: (s) => write(1, text(s)),\n",
+        "println" => "  println: (s) => write(1, text(s) + \"\\n\"),\n",
+        "eprint" => "  eprint: (s) => write(2, text(s)),\n",
+        "eprintln" => "  eprintln: (s) => write(2, text(s) + \"\\n\"),\n",
+        "panic" => {
+            "  panic: (s) => {\n    write(2, `nova: panic: ${text(s)}\\n`);\n    throw new NovaPanic();\n  },\n"
+        }
+        other => bail!("the shim has no host function `{other}`"),
+    })
+}
+
+/// `app.js` for a module importing `imports` from `env`.
+pub fn shim(imports: &[&str]) -> Result<String> {
+    let mut js = String::from(CORE);
+    js.push_str("\n// The host functions the module imports.\nconst env = {\n");
+    for name in imports {
+        js.push_str(host(name)?);
+    }
+    js.push_str("};\n");
+    js.push_str(RUN);
+    Ok(js)
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    use super::*;
+
+    /// Only the imported host functions are in `env`.
+    #[test]
+    fn env_holds_only_the_imported_host_functions() {
+        let js = shim(&["println"]).expect("a shim");
+        assert!(js.contains("  println: (s) =>"), "{js}");
+        assert!(!js.contains("  print: (s) =>"), "{js}");
+        assert!(!js.contains("  panic: (s) =>"), "{js}");
+    }
+
+    /// An import the shim cannot provide is refused, not left undefined.
+    #[test]
+    fn an_unknown_import_is_refused() {
+        let e = shim(&["fetch"]).expect_err("refused");
+        assert!(e.to_string().contains("no host function `fetch`"), "{e}");
+    }
+}
diff --git a/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/core.js b/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/core.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..009bb05
--- /dev/null
+++ b/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/core.js
@@ -0,0 +1,59 @@
+// app.js: generated by `nova build --target wasm`. It loads app.wasm from
+// beside itself, in a browser or under Node, and runs the program's `main`
+// (spec docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md
+// §6). Run directly, `node app.js` runs the program.
+
+const isNode =
+  typeof process === "object" && process.versions != null && process.versions.node != null;
+let wasm;
+const decoder = new TextDecoder();
+const encoder = new TextEncoder();
+
+// A Nova string's text: its header holds the length at 0 and the address of
+// its UTF-8 bytes at 8.
+function text(s) {
+  const view = new DataView(wasm.memory.buffer);
+  const len = Number(view.getBigUint64(s, true));
+  return decoder.decode(new Uint8Array(wasm.memory.buffer, view.getUint32(s + 8, true), len));
+}
+
+// A new Nova string holding `value`, placed by std/rt's allocator: the
+// header, then the bytes inline at 16.
+function string(value) {
+  const bytes = encoder.encode(value);
+  const s = Number(wasm.nova_alloc(BigInt(16 + Math.max(bytes.length, 1))));
+  const view = new DataView(wasm.memory.buffer);
+  view.setBigUint64(s, BigInt(bytes.length), true);
+  view.setUint32(s + 8, s + 16, true);
+  new Uint8Array(wasm.memory.buffer, s + 16, bytes.length).set(bytes);
+  return s;
+}
+
+// Standard output (1) and error (2): Node's streams, or else the console,
+// one whole line at a time.
+const pending = ["", "", ""];
+function write(fd, s) {
+  if (isNode) {
+    (fd === 1 ? process.stdout : process.stderr).write(s);
+    return;
+  }
+  pending[fd] += s;
+  let at;
+  while ((at = pending[fd].indexOf("\n")) >= 0) {
+    (fd === 1 ? console.log : console.error)(pending[fd].slice(0, at));
+    pending[fd] = pending[fd].slice(at + 1);
+  }
+}
+
+// The part of a line still unprinted when `main` returns.
+function flush() {
+  for (const fd of [1, 2]) {
+    if (pending[fd] !== "") {
+      (fd === 1 ? console.log : console.error)(pending[fd]);
+      pending[fd] = "";
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+// What `panic` throws, after it has written its message.
+class NovaPanic extends Error {}

new file mode 100644

--- /dev/null
+++ b/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/index.html

diff --git a/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/run.js b/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/run.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d50ea2f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/run.js
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+
+async function load(options) {
+  const imports = { env };
+  if (options.wasm) return WebAssembly.instantiate(options.wasm, imports);
+  const url = new URL("app.wasm", import.meta.url);
+  if (isNode) {
+    const { readFile } = await import("node:fs/promises");
+    return WebAssembly.instantiate(await readFile(url), imports);
+  }
+  return WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming(fetch(url), imports);
+}
+
+// Load app.wasm and run `main`. A panic or a trap ends the program as a
+// native build ends: its message on stderr and, under Node, the exit status
+// of an abort (134) or of a trap (132).
+export default async function init(options = {}) {
+  const { instance } = await load(options);
+  wasm = instance.exports;
+  try {
+    wasm.main();
+  } catch (e) {
+    let status;
+    if (e instanceof NovaPanic) {
+      status = 134;
+    } else if (e instanceof RangeError && /call stack/i.test(e.message)) {
+      write(2, "nova: stack overflow\n");
+      status = 134;
+    } else if (e instanceof WebAssembly.RuntimeError) {
+      write(2, `nova: trap: ${e.message}\n`);
+      status = 132;
+    } else {
+      throw e;
+    }
+    if (isNode) process.exitCode = status;
+  } finally {
+    flush();
+  }
+}
+
+if (isNode && process.argv[1]) {
+  const { pathToFileURL } = await import("node:url");
+  const { resolve } = await import("node:path");
+  if (import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(resolve(process.argv[1])).href) await init();
+}
diff --git a/crates/nova-driver/src/lib.rs b/crates/nova-driver/src/lib.rs
index 94726df..1f5f165 100644
--- a/crates/nova-driver/src/lib.rs
+++ b/crates/nova-driver/src/lib.rs
@@ -174,6 +174,34 @@ pub fn build_program(program: Program, output: &Path) -> Result<Outcome<PathBuf>
     Ok(Outcome::Ok(output.to_path_buf()))
 }
 
+/// Build `program` for WASM into `out_dir`: `index.html`, `app.js` and
+/// `app.wasm`, replacing those three and leaving the rest of the directory
+/// alone (spec
+/// `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md`
+/// §7).
+pub fn build_program_wasm(program: Program, out_dir: &Path) -> Result<Outcome<PathBuf>> {
+    let mir = match lower_to_mir_for(program, Target::Wasm)? {
+        Outcome::Ok(mir) => mir,
+        Outcome::Failed { errors } => return Ok(Outcome::Failed { errors }),
+    };
+    let output = nova_codegen_wasm::compile_module(&mir)
+        .context("internal WASM codegen error (this is a compiler bug)")?;
+    let app_js = nova_codegen_wasm::shim(&output.imports)
+        .context("internal WASM codegen error (this is a compiler bug)")?;
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(out_dir)
+        .with_context(|| format!("failed to create {}", out_dir.display()))?;
+    for (name, bytes) in [
+        ("index.html", nova_codegen_wasm::INDEX_HTML.as_bytes()),
+        ("app.js", app_js.as_bytes()),
+        ("app.wasm", output.wasm.as_slice()),
+    ] {
+        let path = out_dir.join(name);
+        std::fs::write(&path, bytes)
+            .with_context(|| format!("failed to write {}", path.display()))?;
+    }
+    Ok(Outcome::Ok(out_dir.to_path_buf()))
+}
+
 /// An intermediate-file path derived from `output` that can never alias it:
 /// the full output file name plus `.nova.<ext>` (so even `-o out.ll` /
 /// `-o out.obj` yield a distinct `out.ll.nova.ll` / `out.obj.nova.obj` and the
@@ -622,11 +650,16 @@ fn synthesize_test_main(main_id: DefId, tests: &[TestFn]) -> hir::Function {
 
 /// Run the front end and MIR lowering, rendering any diagnostics.
 fn lower_to_mir(program: Program) -> Result<Outcome<nova_mir::Module>> {
-    let mut ctx = FrontendContext::new(program)?;
+    lower_to_mir_for(program, Target::Native)
+}
+
+/// [`lower_to_mir`] for `target`.
````

  WASM, with ``error[E0091]: this program needs `check_bounds`, which
  target wasm does not have``. The differential one also names
  `bool_to_str`, `char_to_str` and the rest.
- [ ] **Step 4: The code.** `patch_block t7-code`.
  - `std/rt/lib.nova` gains the other 22 runtime functions of spec
    §4.6's `std/rt` kind:
    - `rt_bool_to_str`, `rt_char_to_str`;
    - `rt_str_eq`, `rt_str_cmp`, `rt_str_hash`;
    - `rt_str_len_chars`, `rt_str_chars`, `rt_str_from_chars`;
    - `rt_str_join`, `rt_str_index_of`;
    - `rt_json_quote`, `rt_check_bounds`;
    - and the ten `rt_bytes_*`.
  - Their helpers come too:
    - `str_copy`, `ascii`, `scalar`;
    - `utf8_len`, `utf8_put`, `mem_eq`;
    - `shr`, `splitmix`, `str_seed`;
    - `chars_in`, `json_extra`, `hex_digit`, `continues`.
  - Each is `nova-runtime`'s function written again: the same results,
    and the same panic messages.
- [ ] **Step 5: Run.**
  - The same command. Expected: `15 passed`.
  - `cargo test -p nova-std`. Expected: `2 passed`.
- [ ] **Step 6: Checks,** as Task 1's Step 5.

**Patch `t7-tests`:**

<!-- patch t7-tests -->

````diff
diff --git a/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs b/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
index 578d4c6..f193d27 100644
--- a/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
+++ b/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
@@ -325,3 +325,45 @@ fn a_trap_exits_132() {
     let a = run_wasm(node, &dist).code(132);
     assert_eq!(stderr(&a), "nova: trap: divide by zero\n");
 }
+
+/// A program in `tests/wasm/`, held to its `.stdout` file natively and,
+/// built for WASM, under Node: the two must print the same (spec §9.3).
+fn differential(name: &str) {
+    let source = repo_root().join("tests/wasm").join(format!("{name}.nova"));
+    let expected = std::fs::read_to_string(source.with_extension("stdout"))
+        .expect("the expected output")
+        .replace("\r\n", "\n");
+    let dir = fresh(&format!("diff-{name}"));
+    let native = nova_in(&dir, &["run", &source.to_string_lossy()]).success();
+    assert_eq!(stdout(&native), expected, "natively");
+    let Some(node) = node() else { return };
+    let dist = build_wasm(&dir, &source);
+    let wasm = run_wasm(node, &dist).success();
+    assert_eq!(stdout(&wasm), expected, "on WASM");
+}
+
+/// `std/rt`'s string and bytes functions are `nova-runtime`'s (spec §5.3).
+#[test]
+fn std_rt_strings_and_bytes_match_nova_runtime() {
+    differential("strings_and_bytes");
+}
+
+/// `check_bounds` panics with `nova-runtime`'s message (spec §5.3).
+#[test]
+fn an_index_out_of_bounds_panics_as_natively() {
+    let Some(node) = node() else { return };
+    let dir = fresh("bounds");
+    write(
+        &dir,
+        &[(
+            "main.nova",
+            "fn main() {\n    let xs = [1, 2, 3]\n    let i = xs.len() + 2\n    println(\"${xs[i]}\")\n}\n",
+        )],
+    );
+    let dist = build_wasm(&dir, Path::new("main.nova"));
+    let a = run_wasm(node, &dist).code(134);
+    assert_eq!(
+        stderr(&a),
+        "nova: panic: array index 5 out of bounds for length 3\n"
+    );
+}
diff --git a/tests/wasm/strings_and_bytes.nova b/tests/wasm/strings_and_bytes.nova
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ebb3e1e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/wasm/strings_and_bytes.nova
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+// The string and bytes functions of std/rt, held to nova-runtime's results:
+// built natively and for WASM, this prints the same (spec
+// docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md §9.3).
+
+fn order(a: String, b: String) -> String {
+    match a.cmp(b) {
+        Less => "less"
+        Equal => "equal"
+        Greater => "greater"
+    }
+}
+
+fn show(o: Option<Int>) -> String {
+    match o {
+        Some(v) => "${v}"
+        None => "none"
+    }
+}
+
+fn text(b: Bytes) -> String {
+    match b.to_string() {
+        Some(s) => "'${s}'"
+        None => "not UTF-8"
+    }
+}
+
+fn main() {
+    // Ints, Bools and Chars as text.
+    println("${0} ${-1} ${42} ${9223372036854775807} ${-9223372036854775807 - 1}")
+    println("${true} ${false}")
+    println("${'a'} ${'é'} ${'€'} ${'😀'}")
+
+    // Comparing and measuring.
+    let words = ["", "a", "héllo", "€uro", "😀!"]
+    for i in 0..words.len() {
+        let w = words[i]
+        println("[${w}] len ${w.len()} chars ${w.chars().len()} rev [${w.reverse()}]")
+    }
+    println("${order("a", "b")} ${order("b", "a")} ${order("ab", "a")} ${order("", "")}")
+    println("${order("é", "z")} ${"abc" == "abc"} ${"abc" == "abd"} ${"" == ""}")
+
+    // Searching, slicing and joining.
+    let s = "naïve café, naïve"
+    println("${show(s.index_of("café"))} ${show(s.index_of("x"))} ${show(s.index_of(""))}")
+    println("${s.contains("ïv")} ${s.starts_with("naï")} ${s.ends_with("ve")}")
+    println("[${s.slice(2, 7)}] [${s.slice(12, 17)}]")
+    println("[${", ".join(["a", "é", "€"])}] [${"-".join([])}] [${"".join(["x", "y"])}]")
+    println("${"a,b,,c".split(",").len()} [${"€".repeat(3)}]")
+    let cs = "a€😀".chars()
+    for i in 0..cs.len() { print("${cs[i]}|") }
+    println("")
+
+    // Bytes.
+    let b = bytes_from_string("h€!")
+    println("${b.len()} ${show(b.byte_at(1))} ${show(b.byte_at(5))} ${show(b.byte_at(-1))}")
+    println("${b.slice(1, 4).len()} ${b.slice(3, 1).len()} ${b.slice(-5, 99).len()}")
+    println("${text(b)} ${text(b.slice(0, 2))} ${text(b.concat(bytes_from_string("?")))}")
+    println(
+        "${b.to_ints().len()} ${b.eq(bytes_from_string("h€!"))} ${b.eq(bytes_from_ints([104]))}",
+    )
+    // Overlong, a surrogate, past U+10FFFF, cut short, four bytes, ASCII.
+    let samples = [
+        [0xc0, 0x80],
+        [0xed, 0xa0, 0x80],
+        [0xf4, 0x90, 0x80, 0x80],
+        [0xe2, 0x82],
+        [0xf0, 0x9f, 0x98, 0x80],
+        [0x7f],
+    ]
+    for i in 0..samples.len() { print("${text(bytes_from_ints(samples[i]))};") }
+    println("")
+}
````

**Patch `t7-code`:**

<!-- patch t7-code -->

````diff
diff --git a/std/rt/lib.nova b/std/rt/lib.nova
index 92656ad..adfc373 100644
--- a/std/rt/lib.nova
+++ b/std/rt/lib.nova
@@ -114,3 +114,434 @@ fn rt_int_to_str(v: Int) -> Int {
     if negative { mem_store8(start, 45) }
     h
 }
+
+// A copy of the string or bytes `s`: `gc_str` and `gc_bytes`, which copy.
+fn str_copy(s: Int) -> Int {
+    let len = str_len(s)
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(len)
+    mem_copy(str_bytes(h), str_bytes(s), len)
+    h
+}
+
+// A new string of the ASCII bytes `b0..b4`, of which the first `n` count.
+fn ascii(n: Int, b0: Int, b1: Int, b2: Int, b3: Int, b4: Int) -> Int {
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(n)
+    let at = str_bytes(h)
+    mem_store8(at, b0)
+    if n > 1 { mem_store8(at + 1, b1) }
+    if n > 2 { mem_store8(at + 2, b2) }
+    if n > 3 { mem_store8(at + 3, b3) }
+    if n > 4 { mem_store8(at + 4, b4) }
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_bool_to_str`.
+fn rt_bool_to_str(v: Bool) -> Int {
+    if v { ascii(4, 116, 114, 117, 101, 0) } else { ascii(5, 102, 97, 108, 115, 101) }
+}
+
+// `char::from_u32(v as u32)`, or U+FFFD where that is `None`.
+fn scalar(v: Int) -> Int {
+    let c = v & 4294967295
+    if c > 1114111 || (c >= 55296 && c <= 57343) { 65533 } else { c }
+}
+
+// How many bytes UTF-8 takes for the scalar value `c`.
+fn utf8_len(c: Int) -> Int {
+    if c < 128 { 1 } else if c < 2048 { 2 } else if c < 65536 { 3 } else { 4 }
+}
+
+// Write `c` as UTF-8 at `at`, returning the address after it.
+fn utf8_put(at: Int, c: Int) -> Int {
+    if c < 128 {
+        mem_store8(at, c)
+        at + 1
+    } else if c < 2048 {
+        mem_store8(at, 192 | (c >> 6))
+        mem_store8(at + 1, 128 | (c & 63))
+        at + 2
+    } else if c < 65536 {
+        mem_store8(at, 224 | (c >> 12))
+        mem_store8(at + 1, 128 | ((c >> 6) & 63))
+        mem_store8(at + 2, 128 | (c & 63))
+        at + 3
+    } else {
+        mem_store8(at, 240 | (c >> 18))
+        mem_store8(at + 1, 128 | ((c >> 12) & 63))
+        mem_store8(at + 2, 128 | ((c >> 6) & 63))
+        mem_store8(at + 3, 128 | (c & 63))
+        at + 4
+    }
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_char_to_str`.
+fn rt_char_to_str(v: Int) -> Int {
+    let c = scalar(v)
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(utf8_len(c))
+    utf8_put(str_bytes(h), c)
+    h
+}
+
+// Whether the `n` bytes at `p` and at `q` are equal.
+fn mem_eq(p: Int, q: Int, n: Int) -> Bool {
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        if mem_load8(p + i) != mem_load8(q + i) { return false }
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    true
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_eq`, and `nova_rt_bytes_eq`.
+fn rt_str_eq(a: Int, b: Int) -> Bool {
+    let n = str_len(a)
+    if n != str_len(b) { return false }
+    mem_eq(str_bytes(a), str_bytes(b), n)
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_cmp`: byte by byte, then the shorter first.
+fn rt_str_cmp(a: Int, b: Int) -> Int {
+    let la = str_len(a)
+    let lb = str_len(b)
+    let mut n = la
+    if lb < n { n = lb }
+    let pa = str_bytes(a)
+    let pb = str_bytes(b)
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        let x = mem_load8(pa + i)
+        let y = mem_load8(pb + i)
+        if x < y { return -1 }
+        if x > y { return 1 }
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    if la < lb { -1 } else if la > lb { 1 } else { 0 }
+}
+
+// `z >> k` as an unsigned shift: Nova's `>>` keeps the sign.
+fn shr(z: Int, k: Int) -> Int { (z >> k) & ((1 << (64 - k)) - 1) }
+
+// `nova-runtime`'s `splitmix64_finalize`.
+fn splitmix(value: Int) -> Int {
+    let mut z = value
+    z = z ^ shr(z, 30)
+    z = z * -4658895280553007687
+    z = z ^ shr(z, 27)
+    z = z * -7723592293110705685
+    z ^ shr(z, 31)
+}
+
+// The state words of `str_hash`'s seed: the seed, and whether it is drawn.
+const STR_SEED: Int = 16
+
+const STR_SEED_DRAWN: Int = 24
+
+// `str_hash_seed`: drawn once per run from the shim's random seed, kept
+// apart from `int_hash_seed`'s own value, as the native seeds are apart.
+fn str_seed() -> Int {
+    if mem_load64(STR_SEED_DRAWN) == 0 {
+        mem_store64(STR_SEED, splitmix(int_hash_seed() ^ 6148914691236517205))
+        mem_store64(STR_SEED_DRAWN, 1)
+    }
+    mem_load64(STR_SEED)
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_hash`: seeded FNV-1a over the bytes, then the finalizer.
+fn rt_str_hash(s: Int) -> Int {
+    let mut h = str_seed()
+    let p = str_bytes(s)
+    let n = str_len(s)
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        h = (h ^ mem_load8(p + i)) * 1099511628211
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    splitmix(h)
+}
+
+// How many characters the `n` bytes of UTF-8 at `p` hold: the bytes that
+// do not continue one.
+fn chars_in(p: Int, n: Int) -> Int {
+    let mut count = 0
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        if (mem_load8(p + i) & 192) != 128 { count = count + 1 }
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    count
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_len_chars`.
+fn rt_str_len_chars(s: Int) -> Int { chars_in(str_bytes(s), str_len(s)) }
+
+// `nova_rt_str_chars`: the string's characters, as an array of scalar
+// values. The string is valid UTF-8.
+fn rt_str_chars(s: Int) -> Int {
+    let p = str_bytes(s)
+    let len = str_len(s)
+    let n = chars_in(p, len)
+    let arr = rt_alloc(8 + 8 * n)
+    mem_store64(arr, n)
+    let mut i = 0
+    let mut k = 0
+    while i < len {
+        let b = mem_load8(p + i)
+        let mut c = b
+        let mut width = 1
+        if b >= 240 {
+            c = ((b & 7) << 18) | ((mem_load8(p + i + 1) & 63) << 12)
+            c = c | ((mem_load8(p + i + 2) & 63) << 6) | (mem_load8(p + i + 3) & 63)
+            width = 4
+        } else if b >= 224 {
+            c = ((b & 15) << 12) | ((mem_load8(p + i + 1) & 63) << 6)
+            c = c | (mem_load8(p + i + 2) & 63)
+            width = 3
+        } else if b >= 192 {
+            c = ((b & 31) << 6) | (mem_load8(p + i + 1) & 63)
+            width = 2
+        }
+        mem_store64(arr + 8 + 8 * k, c)
+        k = k + 1
+        i = i + width
+    }
+    arr
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_from_chars`: an invalid value becomes U+FFFD, and a negative
+// length counts as zero.
+fn rt_str_from_chars(chars: Int) -> Int {
+    let mut n = mem_load64(chars)
+    if n < 0 { n = 0 }
+    let mut total = 0
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        total = total + utf8_len(scalar(mem_load64(chars + 8 + 8 * i)))
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(total)
+    let mut at = str_bytes(h)
+    i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        at = utf8_put(at, scalar(mem_load64(chars + 8 + 8 * i)))
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_join`: the parts, with `sep` between each pair.
+fn rt_str_join(sep: Int, parts: Int) -> Int {
+    let mut n = mem_load64(parts)
+    if n < 0 { n = 0 }
+    let ls = str_len(sep)
+    let mut total = 0
+    if n > 0 { total = ls * (n - 1) }
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        total = total + str_len(mem_load64(parts + 8 + 8 * i))
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(total)
+    let mut at = str_bytes(h)
+    i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        if i > 0 {
+            mem_copy(at, str_bytes(sep), ls)
+            at = at + ls
+        }
+        let part = mem_load64(parts + 8 + 8 * i)
+        let len = str_len(part)
+        mem_copy(at, str_bytes(part), len)
+        at = at + len
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_str_index_of`: the character index of the first `needle` in
+// `haystack`, or -1. A byte match starts on a character boundary, since no
+// character starts with a continuation byte.
+fn rt_str_index_of(haystack: Int, needle: Int) -> Int {
+    let h = str_bytes(haystack)
+    let lh = str_len(haystack)
+    let n = str_bytes(needle)
+    let ln = str_len(needle)
+    let mut at = 0
+    while at + ln <= lh {
+        if mem_eq(h + at, n, ln) { return chars_in(h, at) }
+        at = at + 1
+    }
+    // Not `-1` on a line of its own, which would continue the `while`.
+    0 - 1
+}
+
+// The extra bytes byte `b` takes in a JSON string literal.
+fn json_extra(b: Int) -> Int {
+    if b == 34 || b == 92 || b == 10 || b == 13 || b == 9 || b == 8 || b == 12 {
+        1
+    } else if b < 32 {
+        5
+    } else {
+        0
+    }
+}
+
+// The letter of a hex digit, lowercase.
+fn hex_digit(d: Int) -> Int { if d < 10 { 48 + d } else { 87 + d } }
+
+// `nova_rt_json_quote`: `s` as a JSON string literal, quotes included
+// (RFC 8259).
+fn rt_json_quote(s: Int) -> Int {
+    let p = str_bytes(s)
+    let n = str_len(s)
+    let mut extra = 0
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        extra = extra + json_extra(mem_load8(p + i))
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(n + 2 + extra)
+    let mut at = str_bytes(h)
+    mem_store8(at, 34)
+    at = at + 1
+    i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        let b = mem_load8(p + i)
+        let e = json_extra(b)
+        if e == 0 {
+            mem_store8(at, b)
+            at = at + 1
+        } else if e == 1 {
+            mem_store8(at, 92)
+            let mut letter = b
+            if b == 10 { letter = 110 }
+            if b == 13 { letter = 114 }
+            if b == 9 { letter = 116 }
+            if b == 8 { letter = 98 }
+            if b == 12 { letter = 102 }
+            mem_store8(at + 1, letter)
+            at = at + 2
+        } else {
+            mem_store8(at, 92)
+            mem_store8(at + 1, 117)
+            mem_store8(at + 2, 48)
+            mem_store8(at + 3, 48)
+            mem_store8(at + 4, hex_digit(b >> 4))
+            mem_store8(at + 5, hex_digit(b & 15))
+            at = at + 6
+        }
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    mem_store8(at, 34)
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_check_bounds`.
+fn rt_check_bounds(index: Int, len: Int) {
+    if index < 0 || index >= len { panic("array index ${index} out of bounds for length ${len}") }
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_len`.
+fn rt_bytes_len(b: Int) -> Int { str_len(b) }
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_from_string`.
+fn rt_bytes_from_string(s: Int) -> Int { str_copy(s) }
+
+// Whether byte `b` continues a UTF-8 character.
+fn continues(b: Int) -> Bool { b >= 128 && b <= 191 }
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_is_utf8`: `std::str::from_utf8`'s rules. No overlong form,
+// no surrogate, nothing past U+10FFFF.
+fn rt_bytes_is_utf8(b: Int) -> Bool {
+    let p = str_bytes(b)
+    let n = str_len(b)
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        let b0 = mem_load8(p + i)
+        if b0 < 128 {
+            i = i + 1
+        } else {
+            let mut width = 0
+            let mut lo = 128
+            let mut hi = 191
+            if b0 >= 194 && b0 <= 223 { width = 2 }
+            if b0 >= 224 && b0 <= 239 { width = 3 }
+            if b0 >= 240 && b0 <= 244 { width = 4 }
+            if b0 == 224 { lo = 160 }
+            if b0 == 237 { hi = 159 }
+            if b0 == 240 { lo = 144 }
+            if b0 == 244 { hi = 143 }
+            if width == 0 || i + width > n { return false }
+            let b1 = mem_load8(p + i + 1)
+            if b1 < lo || b1 > hi { return false }
+            let mut k = 2
+            while k < width {
+                if !continues(mem_load8(p + i + k)) { return false }
+                k = k + 1
+            }
+            i = i + width
+        }
+    }
+    true
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_to_string_unchecked`.
+fn rt_bytes_to_string_unchecked(b: Int) -> Int { str_copy(b) }
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_at`.
+fn rt_bytes_at(b: Int, i: Int) -> Int {
+    if i < 0 { panic("nova_rt_bytes_at: negative index") }
+    if i >= str_len(b) { panic("nova_rt_bytes_at: index out of range") }
+    mem_load8(str_bytes(b) + i)
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_slice`: the bounds clamped to `0..len`, and empty when
+// they cross.
+fn rt_bytes_slice(b: Int, start: Int, end: Int) -> Int {
+    let len = str_len(b)
+    let mut lo = start
+    if lo < 0 { lo = 0 }
+    if lo > len { lo = len }
+    let mut hi = end
+    if hi < 0 { hi = 0 }
+    if hi > len { hi = len }
+    if hi <= lo { return rt_str_alloc(0) }
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(hi - lo)
+    mem_copy(str_bytes(h), str_bytes(b) + lo, hi - lo)
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_concat`.
+fn rt_bytes_concat(a: Int, b: Int) -> Int { rt_str_concat(a, b) }
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_to_ints`.
+fn rt_bytes_to_ints(b: Int) -> Int {
+    let n = str_len(b)
+    let p = str_bytes(b)
+    let arr = rt_alloc(8 + 8 * n)
+    mem_store64(arr, n)
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        mem_store64(arr + 8 + 8 * i, mem_load8(p + i))
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    arr
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_from_ints`: each element must be a byte.
+fn rt_bytes_from_ints(ints: Int) -> Int {
+    let mut n = mem_load64(ints)
+    if n < 0 { n = 0 }
+    let h = rt_str_alloc(n)
+    let at = str_bytes(h)
+    let mut i = 0
+    while i < n {
+        let v = mem_load64(ints + 8 + 8 * i)
+        if v < 0 || v > 255 { panic("nova_rt_bytes_from_ints: element out of range 0..=255") }
+        mem_store8(at + i, v)
+        i = i + 1
+    }
+    h
+}
+
+// `nova_rt_bytes_eq`.
+fn rt_bytes_eq(a: Int, b: Int) -> Bool { rt_str_eq(a, b) }
````

### Task 8: The shim's host functions; the gate

Spec §6, §9.2 and §9.3; decisions 21 and 22.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim/float.js`
- Modify: `crates/nova-codegen-wasm/src/shim.rs`
- Test:
  - `crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs`
  - `tests/wasm/{float_text,float_fixed,case_and_hash}.nova`
  - the three `.stdout` files, generated

- [ ] **Step 1: The tests.** `patch_block t8-tests`.
  - Three differential tests:
    - `float_text_matches_nova_runtime`;
    - `float_fixed_matches_nova_runtime`;
    - `case_and_hash_match_nova_runtime`.
  - Their programs:
    - `float_text.nova`: the shortest text of `Float`s, the exact ties
      among them included;
    - `float_fixed.nova`: fixed precision from -1 to 18 places on exact
      ties (`2.5`, `0.125`), near ones (`2.675`, `0.1`), the extremes
      and the specials, then every sixteenth from -12.5 to 12.4375;
    - `case_and_hash.nova`: case mapping beyond ASCII (`straße`,
      `İstanbul`, `ǅungla`, `ﬁne`), and hashing: equal strings hash the
      same within a run, and a map of 100 keys finds what it holds.

    Text read back as a `Float` is checked by the gate's
    `json_parse_numbers`.
  - **The gate, as `mod gate`:**
    - `gate_program(name)` builds `tests/runtime/<name>.nova` for WASM
      and holds it to its `.stdout` file;
    - `gate!` makes the 41 such tests spec §9.2 lists;
    - `gate_panics` checks the five panicking programs:
      - `panic_unwrap`, `bytes_from_ints_too_high` and
        `bytes_from_ints_too_low`;
      - `json_render_guard` and `json_stringify_cycle`.

      Each must fail with its native message and status 134; what it
      printed first must pass the check `run_tests.rs` makes of the native
      build; and it must end by its own panic, not by a stack overflow or
      a trap.
- [ ] **Step 2: The expected outputs,** from the native build:

  ```sh
  for f in float_text float_fixed case_and_hash; do
    cargo run --locked -q -p nova-cli -- run tests/wasm/$f.nova > tests/wasm/$f.stdout
  done
  sha256sum tests/wasm/float_text.stdout tests/wasm/float_fixed.stdout tests/wasm/case_and_hash.stdout
  ```

  Expected:
  - `daa3885f7483b489d40fa3c0350523e2875a3fc339de6e12995814a448496900`;
  - `e1aa8d785f5d958acd302d7d61c5ea1c00dcc5703dfb58e530648ade3f6a047f`;
  - `e2eb478d9d3daa48d4040f60b779bab3d55dd4febd7b80e862ba682df990d523`.
- [ ] **Step 3: Run, and see them fail.**
  `NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE=1 cargo test -p nova-cli --test wasm_tests`.
  Expected: `39 passed; 25 failed`:
  - the three differential tests;
  - 20 of the 41 stdout programs, among them every JSON program,
    `collections`, `map_keys`, `strings` and `fmt_float_fixed`;
  - two of the panicking ones, `json_render_guard` and
    `json_stringify_cycle`.

  Each stops building with ``the shim has no host function `…` ``,
  naming `int_hash_seed`, `float_to_str`, `float_fixed`, `str_to_float`
  or `str_to_lower`.
- [ ] **Step 4: The code.** `patch_block t8-code`.
  - **`float.js`:**
    - `exactly(v)` and `shortest(v)`, on `BigInt`s;
    - `floatText(v)`: Rust's `Display`, shortest and never an exponent.
      Where two shortest texts are equally near, it takes the larger, as
      Rust does: JS takes the even one;
    - `floatFixed(v, places)`: Rust's `{:.N}`, the exact value rounded
      half to even, with `places` clamped to 0..=17;
    - `floatFrom(s)`: Rust's `f64` grammar exactly, then `Number`, or 0.0
      where the grammar fails.
  - **`shim.rs`:**
    - the other six entries: `float_to_str`, `float_fixed`,
      `str_to_float`, `str_to_upper`, `str_to_lower`, `int_hash_seed`;
    - `float.js` comes only with a `Float` import, and the seed only with
      `int_hash_seed`. The seed is drawn once per run, from
      `crypto.getRandomValues`;
    - two unit tests: `helpers_come_only_with_their_host_functions`;
      and `float_from_is_rusts_parse` (decision 24), which runs
      `floatFrom` under Node over 69 texts, every case of spec §6's
      grammar valid and not, and compares the bits with Rust's
      `str::parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(0.0)`. It writes its script to a
      fixed directory under the system's temporary directory, and needs
      Node as the CLI tests do. Task 10's mutant 14 shows that it bites.
- [ ] **Step 5: Run.**
  - The same command. Expected: `64 passed`. The slowest test,
    `gate::json_render_guard_under`, takes about a quarter of a second.
  - `NOVA_REQUIRE_NODE=1 cargo test -p nova-codegen-wasm`. Expected:
    `23 passed`.
- [ ] **Step 6: Checks,** as Task 1's Step 5, without `nova fmt`.
- [ ] **Step 7: Commit**
  `wasm: the shim's Float, case and seed functions; the gate's 46 programs under Node`,
  with the three `.stdout` files.

````diff
diff --git a/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs b/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
index f193d27..9bffa51 100644
--- a/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
+++ b/crates/nova-cli/tests/wasm_tests.rs
@@ -367,3 +367,169 @@ fn an_index_out_of_bounds_panics_as_natively() {
         "nova: panic: array index 5 out of bounds for length 3\n"
     );
 }
+
+/// The shim's `Float` text is `nova-runtime`'s, exact ties included (spec
+/// §6, §9.3).
+#[test]
+fn float_text_matches_nova_runtime() {
+    differential("float_text");
+}
+
+/// The shim's `Float::fixed` rounds as `nova-runtime`'s does, half to even
+/// on the exact value (spec §6, §9.3).
+#[test]
+fn float_fixed_matches_nova_runtime() {
+    differential("float_fixed");
+}
+
+/// Case mapping and hashing on WASM agree with `nova-runtime` (spec §6,
+/// §9.3).
+#[test]
+fn case_and_hash_match_nova_runtime() {
+    differential("case_and_hash");
+}
+
+/// One of the gate's programs (spec §9.2): built for WASM, it prints under
+/// Node what its `.stdout` file says the native build prints.
+fn gate_program(name: &str) {
+    let Some(node) = node() else { return };
+    let source = repo_root()
+        .join("tests/runtime")
+        .join(format!("{name}.nova"));
+    let expected = std::fs::read_to_string(source.with_extension("stdout"))
+        .expect("the expected output")
+        .replace("\r\n", "\n");
+    let dir = fresh(&format!("gate-{name}"));
+    let dist = build_wasm(&dir, &source);
+    let a = run_wasm(node, &dist).success();
+    assert_eq!(stdout(&a), expected);
+}
+
+/// One of the gate's panicking programs: it fails with the native build's
+/// message on stderr and an abort's status, as `run_tests.rs` checks it
+/// natively, and what it printed first passes `printed`. It ends by its
+/// own panic, not by a stack overflow or a trap.
+fn gate_panics(name: &str, message: &str, printed: fn(&str) -> bool) {
+    let Some(node) = node() else { return };
+    let source = repo_root()
+        .join("tests/runtime")
+        .join(format!("{name}.nova"));
+    let dir = fresh(&format!("gate-{name}"));
+    let dist = build_wasm(&dir, &source);
+    let a = run_wasm(node, &dist).code(134);
+    let err = stderr(&a);
+    assert!(err.contains(message), "stderr: {err}");
+    assert!(
+        !err.contains("stack overflow") && !err.contains("nova: trap"),
+        "stderr: {err}"
+    );
+    assert!(printed(&stdout(&a)), "stdout: {}", stdout(&a));
+}
+
+macro_rules! gate {
+    ($($name:ident),+ $(,)?) => {
+        $(
+            #[test]
+            fn $name() {
+                super::gate_program(stringify!($name));
+            }
+        )+
+    };
+}
+
+/// The gate's `tests/runtime/` programs, by behaviour (spec §9.2).
+mod gate {
+    gate!(
+        // Control flow and constants.
+        break_continue,
+        constants,
+        for_loops,
+        // Data.
+        array_repeat,
+        arrays,
+        collections,
+        field_assign,
+        map_keys,
+        match_enum,
+        records,
+        // Functions, generics and traits.
+        assoc_fn,
+        assoc_types,
+        closures,
+        generic_impls,
+        generic_trait_methods,
+        generics,
+        iterator,
+        method_generics,
+        supertraits,
+        trait_assoc_fn,
+        traits,
+        where_clauses,
+        // std.
+        prelude_option_result,
+        std_core,
+        strings,
+        // Bytes.
+        bytes_basics,
+        // Formatting.
+        fmt_float_edge,
+        fmt_float_fixed,
+        fmt_int_pad,
+        fmt_string_pad,
+        // JSON.
+        json_depth_empty,
+        json_depth_leaf,
+        json_object_forged_map,
+        json_parse_numbers,
+        json_parse_strings,
+        json_parse_values,
+        json_render_deep,
+        json_render_guard_under,
+        json_round_trip,
+        json_stringify,
+        json_stringify_escapes,
+    );
+
+    #[test]
+    fn panic_unwrap() {
+        super::gate_panics("panic_unwrap", "nova: panic: called get on None", |out| {
+            out.contains('7') && !out.contains("unreachable")
+        });
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn bytes_from_ints_too_high() {
+        super::gate_panics(
+            "bytes_from_ints_too_high",
+            "nova: panic: nova_rt_bytes_from_ints: element out of range 0..=255",
+            |out| !out.contains('1'),
+        );
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn bytes_from_ints_too_low() {
+        super::gate_panics(
+            "bytes_from_ints_too_low",
+            "nova: panic: nova_rt_bytes_from_ints: element out of range 0..=255",
+            |out| !out.contains('1'),
+        );
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn json_render_guard() {
+        super::gate_panics(
+            "json_render_guard",
+            "nova: panic: stringify: nesting too deep or cyclic value",
+            |out| out == "built, about to render\n",
+        );
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn json_stringify_cycle() {
+        super::gate_panics(
+            "json_stringify_cycle",
+            "nova: panic: stringify: nesting too deep or cyclic value",
+            |out| out == "about to render a cyclic value\n",
+        );
+    }
+}
diff --git a/tests/wasm/case_and_hash.nova b/tests/wasm/case_and_hash.nova
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5eee42a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/wasm/case_and_hash.nova
@@ -0,0 +1,31 @@
+// Case mapping and string hashing, held to nova-runtime's: built natively
+// and for WASM, this prints the same (spec
+// docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md §9.3).
+// On WASM the shim maps case with JS's full Unicode mappings.
+
+fn main() {
+    let words = [
+        "Hello, World",
+        "straße",
+        "İstanbul",
+        "ΟΔΟΣ",
+        "ǅungla",
+        "Ünïcödé",
+        "ﬁne",
+        "日本語 text",
+        "",
+    ]
+    for i in 0..words.len() {
+        let w = words[i]
+        println("[${w.to_upper()}] [${w.to_lower()}]")
+    }
+    // A hash is the same for equal strings within one run, and a map finds
+    // what it holds.
+    let a = "nova"
+    let half = "va"
+    let b = "no${half}"
+    println("${a.hash() == b.hash()} ${a.hash() == "nova!".hash()}")
+    let mut m = Map::new()
+    for i in 0..100 { m.insert("key${i}", i) }
+    println("${m.len()} ${m.get("key42").unwrap_or(-1)} ${m.get("key100").unwrap_or(-1)}")
+}
diff --git a/tests/wasm/float_fixed.nova b/tests/wasm/float_fixed.nova
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..4328ada
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/wasm/float_fixed.nova
@@ -0,0 +1,42 @@
+// `Float::fixed`, held to nova-runtime's `{:.N}`: built natively and for
+// WASM, this prints the same (spec
+// docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md §9.3).
+// On WASM the shim rounds the exact value half to even.
+
+fn main() {
+    let zero = 0.0
+    let values = [
+        0.125,
+        2.5,
+        1.5,
+        0.5,
+        -0.5,
+        0.375,
+        -0.001,
+        123.456,
+        1.0e20,
+        1.0e21,
+        1.0e22,
+        5.0e-324,
+        0.1,
+        2.675,
+        1.0 / 3.0,
+        -zero,
+        zero / zero,
+        1.0 / zero,
+        -1.0 / zero,
+    ]
+    for i in 0..values.len() {
+        for places in -1..19 { print("${values[i].fixed(places)} ") }
+        println("")
+    }
+    // Every sixteenth from -12.5 to 12.4375: halves, quarters and eighths
+    // are exact ties at some precision.
+    let mut v = -12.5
+    for k in 0..400 {
+        print("${v.fixed(0)} ${v.fixed(1)} ${v.fixed(3)} ")
+        if k % 6 == 5 { println("") }
+        v = v + 0.0625
+    }
+    println("")
+}
diff --git a/tests/wasm/float_text.nova b/tests/wasm/float_text.nova
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..fc9d3bd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/wasm/float_text.nova
@@ -0,0 +1,37 @@
+// `Float`s as text, held to nova-runtime's: built natively and for WASM,
+// this prints the same (spec
+// docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-phase-4-0-foundations-design.md §9.3).
+// On WASM the shim formats them, from JS's shortest digits.
+
+fn main() {
+    // Exact ties between two shortest texts, where ECMAScript and Rust
+    // choose differently (the spike's).
+    println("${1658206780088562.25} ${-1052730259603333.25}")
+    println("${233115890514796.125} ${271821092707313.625}")
+
+    // Specials.
+    let zero = 0.0
+    println("${zero} ${-zero} ${zero / zero} ${1.0 / zero} ${-1.0 / zero}")
+
+    // Quotients and products. Nova has no `Int` to `Float` conversion, so
+    // `x` counts on its own.
+    let mut x = 0.0
+    for i in 0..2000 {
+        print("${x / 7.0} ${x * 0.1} ")
+        if i % 8 == 7 { println("") }
+        x = x + 1.0
+    }
+    println("")
+
+    // Powers of ten and their neighbours, from 1e-320 to 1e308.
+    let mut small = 1.0
+    for _ in 0..320 {
+        small = small / 10.0
+        println("${small} ${small * 1.0000000000000002} ${small * 0.9999999999999999}")
+    }
+    let mut big = 1.0
+    for _ in 0..308 {
+        big = big * 10.0
+        println("${big} ${big * 1.0000000000000002} ${big * 0.9999999999999999}")
+    }
+}
````

````diff
+    fn node() -> Option<std::process::Command> {

+            "nan",
+            "NaN",
````
